Method: 将LLM路由统一表述为序列决策过程，包含上下文编码器、模型编码器、评分函数、决策规则和学习信号五个组件；构建自动化流水线生成路由监督数据并评估响应质量与推理成本；开发开源模块化基础设施LLMRouter，涵盖16种代表性路由器；建立涵盖通用、记忆增强、视觉、时间序列及个性化任务的基准测试xRouteBench。

Result: 学习式路由器相比最强固定模型基线相对提升14.6%；在严格成本约束下，轻量级路由器更具竞争力；用户条件路由持续改善个性化表现。

Conclusion: 统一的LLM路由 formulation 有助于标准化评估与比较，xRouteBench和LLMRouter为后续研究提供了可扩展的基准和工具，证明了动态路由在平衡性能、成本和个性化方面的有效性。

Abstract: No single large language model (LLM) is optimal across all queries and budget constraints, making model routing essential for cost-effective deployment. Existing routers adopt diverse formulations and implementations, making fair comparison and extension difficult. We present a unified formulation of LLM routing as a sequential decision process characterized by five components: context encoders, model encoders, scoring functions, decision rules, and learning signals, covering single-turn, multi-turn, and personalized routing. Based on this formulation, we develop an automated pipeline for constructing routing supervision and evaluating routers jointly on response quality and inference cost. The resulting benchmark, xRouteBench, spans generic LLM, memory-augmented, vision, time-series, and personalized routing tasks. We further introduce LLMRouter, an open-source modular infrastructure with more than 16 representative routers. Our empirical study shows that learned routers outperform the strongest fixed-model baseline by 14.6% relatively, lightweight routers become more competitive under tight cost constraints, and user-conditioned routing consistently improves personalization.

</details>


### [104] [Calibrating WEAT Against Anisotropy: ZCA Whitening as a Geometric Pre-Processing Step for Embedding Association Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06908)
*Seitaro Ono,Senna Ross,Jun Saiki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ZCA白化作为词嵌入关联测试（WEAT）的几何预处理步骤，以解决因嵌入空间各向异性导致的偏差测量不可靠问题。实验表明该方法显著改善空间各向同性，并导致超过30%的WEAT结果显著性状态发生改变，提示需重新评估既往偏差研究结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WEAT依赖余弦相似度衡量语义关联，其前提是嵌入空间近似各向同性。然而，许多常用语言模型不满足此假设，导致现有偏差测量的可靠性受到质疑。

Method: 采用零相位分量分析（ZCA）白化作为几何预处理步骤，将嵌入空间的协方差矩阵转化为单位矩阵，同时最小化对原始向量的扰动，从而恢复各向同性条件。在10个标准WEAT测试套件和7个涵盖三种架构家族的语言模型上进行了70种模型-任务组合的评估。

Result: ZCA白化显著降低了所有模型嵌入空间的各向异性；对于高度各向异性的模型，标准语义相似度基准测试性能也有所提升。校准后，超过30%的WEAT结果发生显著性状态变化，效应量根据偏差类别向不同方向偏移。

Conclusion: 未校准的测量可能高估或低估嵌入空间中编码的关联。此前在各向异性嵌入空间中报告的偏差测量结果应谨慎解读，并建议利用校准方法进行重新评估，以恢复WEAT在计算社会科学和AI公平性研究中的测量基础。

Abstract: We propose Zero-phase Component Analysis (ZCA) whitening as a geometric pre-processing step for the Word Embedding Association Test (WEAT). WEAT is a bias measurement method widely used in both computational social science and AI fairness research. It relies on cosine similarity as a measure of semantic association, which assumes that the embedding space is approximately isotropic. However, prior work has reported that many widely used language models do not satisfy this assumption, raising concerns about the reliability of bias measurements. ZCA whitening transforms the covariance of the embedding space into the identity matrix while minimizing perturbation to the original vectors. This transformation restores the isotropy condition on which WEAT relies. We evaluate our approach on ten standard WEAT test suites and seven models spanning three architectural families, yielding 70 model-task combinations. The results show that ZCA whitening substantially reduces the anisotropy of the embedding spaces across all models. Particularly for highly anisotropic models, we further observe improvements on standard semantic similarity benchmarks, indicating that the calibrated space better captures semantic associations. After calibration, over 30% of WEAT results change significance status, and effect sizes shift in both directions depending on bias category. These shifts suggest that uncalibrated measurements may both overestimate and underestimate the associations encoded in the embedding space. These findings indicate that previously reported bias measurements in anisotropic embedding spaces should be interpreted with caution and may benefit from re-evaluation with calibrated methods. Our approach contributes to restoring the measurement foundation of WEAT across both computational social science and AI fairness research.

</details>


### [105] [Ask-E: An Environment for Calibrated Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06933)
*Sarah Pratt,Jae Sung Park,Scott Geng,Ali Farhadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Ask-E环境，通过让模型生成恰好能被两个基准模型中之一解决的题目来校准难度，以此作为基准和训练手段。该方法无需新数据或强模型交互，即可显著提升模型的数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型改进依赖于在模型能力前沿创建问题，但这需要超越当前模型能力的生成技能，形成负担性约束。作者旨在利用这一约束，将“生成校准问题”的能力转化为提升模型自身能力的途径。

Method: 构建Ask-E环境，定义目标技能水平为两个现有语言模型能力之间的范围。若生成的题目恰好仅能被其中一个模型解决，则视为成功校准。模型在此环境中接受训练，学习生成符合特定难度区间的题目，而非直接回答问题。

Result: 1. 即使是前沿模型在基准测试中的校准成功率也低于50%，表明该基准具有区分度且留有测量未来进步的空间。2. 在该环境下训练模型，能在无新数学数据、无与更强模型交互、无基于正确性的奖励的情况下，显著提升下游多个数学基准任务的表现。

Conclusion: 利用模型生成校准问题的能力作为反馈信号，可以有效提升模型在数学推理等复杂任务上的表现，证明了“通过出题来学习”是一种高效且资源节约的模型增强策略。

Abstract: Today, we improve models by training and evaluating them on problems at the frontier of their abilities. Creating such problems is itself a demanding task, requiring the ability to probe model limits and generalize beyond existing question distributions. It also means placing problems at a precise difficulty level, which requires understanding what it takes to solve them. In short, generating problems calibrated to a model's current frontier demands capability beyond it, an increasingly burdensome constraint as models improve. Our key insight is that we can leverage this constraint to our advantage: a model that can generate problems consistently calibrated to a given frontier must possess capability beyond it. Accordingly, we present Ask-E, an environment that benchmarks and trains models on their ability to write questions at a given skill level, rather than answer them. Concretely, we define target skill levels as ranges bounded by the capabilities of two existing language models. A generated question is successfully calibrated if exactly one of the two models can solve it, placing it precisely within the target range and differentiating the capabilities of these models. Ask-E serves both as a benchmark and a training environment, where models generate problems calibrated to a variety of skill levels. We find that even frontier models achieve below 50% calibration on the benchmark, leaving significant headroom to measure future progress. We also show that training on this environment leads to improvements across a number of downstream math benchmarks even with no new math data, no interaction with stronger models, and no correctness-based reward.

</details>


### [106] [Explicit, Not Longer: What Makes Epistemic Stance Survive Memory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06953)
*Alex Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了在代理记忆系统中，如何保留声明的认识论立场（stance）。发现将立场作为显式标签字段存储比作为括号旁的补充说明能显著提高保留率（约15点），且具体格式效果取决于模型。核心结论是：必须使立场显式化，而非仅增加长度，且最佳方式因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理记忆系统为了压缩存储空间，通常会丢弃修饰语或背景信息，导致声明的“认识论立场”（即对该声明的确信程度或态度）在写入记忆时往往丢失。本研究旨在探究何种机制能够确保这种立场在压缩和存储过程中得以保留。

Method: 研究人员设计了对照实验，比较了两种存储格式对立场保留的影响：一种是将立场作为明确的标记字段（labelled field），另一种是将其作为括号内的旁注（bracketed aside）。实验涉及7种语域下的60个声明，并在两个不同的模型上进行测试。此外，还进行了预注册的复制实验（Haiku模型），并对格式进行了消融分析（ablation study），以区分标签、长度和措辞（完整句子 vs 短语）各自的作用。最后通过确定性读出模型和无模型的手动标注进行验证。

Result: 1. **主要发现**：将立场写为标记字段而非括号旁注，使两个模型上的立场保留率提高了约15分（统计显著，p=0.00005）。预注册复制实验也证实了这一效应（+15.6分）。
2. **消融分析**：标签的使用在两个模型上均有帮助（分别提升9.7和12.8分），而单纯增加长度没有帮助。其中一个模型中，将立场表述为完整句子贡献最大（+12.5分），而在另一个模型中无显著作用（+0.6分）。
3. **一致性**：尽管不同模型的细节机制不同，但交集结论一致：使立场显式化是关键，且最佳显式化方式取决于具体模型。确定性读出模型复现了主要方向和部分对比结果。手动标注者间信度较高（kappa=0.75）。

Conclusion: 在构建代理记忆系统时，为了确保声明的认识论立场不被压缩过程丢弃，必须采用显式化的方式来存储立场（如使用标签字段），而不能仅仅依靠增加文本长度。然而，具体的显式化策略（如是否使用完整句子）应根据所选的记忆模型特性进行调整，没有放之四海而皆准的最优格式。

Abstract: Agent memory systems compress what they store, and compression is built to drop qualifiers, so a claim's epistemic standing tends not to survive being written to memory. We ask what governs whether it does. Matched notes carry the identical claim and identical stance and differ only in where that stance sits; one model compresses both under the same budget among the same filler notes, and a blind reader that never sees the condition scores the result. Across 60 claims in seven registers, writing the stance as a labelled field rather than a bracketed aside raises retention by about 15 points on two models (37 claims to 2 on one, 30 to 8 on the other; permutation p=0.00005), and a pre-registered replication on Haiku, its prediction and decision rule committed before the run, gives +15.6 points, 38 claims to 1. Ablating the format on both models gives the same net effect from different parts: labels help on both (+9.7 and +12.8) and length helps on neither, but wording the stance as a full sentence is the largest component on one model (+12.5) and worth nothing on the other (+0.6). Either model alone would have licensed a confident and different mechanism, so we claim only the intersection: make the stance explicit, not merely longer, and expect the best way of being explicit to depend on the model. A deterministic readout with no model reproduces the two-cell direction and five of seven ablation contrasts, but not length or labels, which we therefore do not claim on one instrument. Fifty hand labels (kappa=0.75) agree on direction; we print their seven disagreements in full. We also report nine withdrawn claims, three of them former title claims of this paper.

</details>


### [107] [Can Language Models Imagine Without Seeing? Ekphrasis: Measuring Visual Creative Ideation in Text-Only LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06967)
*Hongyu Luo,He Wang,Huihao Jing,Hong Ting Tsang,Yuxuan Liu,Wuganjing Song,Yauwai Yim,Chunyang Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Visual Creative Ideation (VCI)概念及Ekphrasis基准，用于评估语言模型在图像生成前产生新颖、有用且富有表现力的视觉文本计划的能力，揭示VCI与文本流畅度分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估无法隔离纯文本语言模型是否在图像生成前真正起源了视觉概念；流畅的视觉散文可能掩盖视觉规划失败（如重复陈词滥调或无法指定可渲染场景）。

Method: 定义VCI为产生有用、有表现力且群体新颖的文本视觉计划的能力；引入Ekphrasis基准（400项任务，涵盖抽象、组合、转换、适应）；通过匿名成对比较、维度特定检查表、Bradley-Terry模型聚合偏好及Typed Idea Graphs将陈词滥调转化为新颖性参考进行评估。

Result: 在14个语言模型中，VCI将有用性、表现力和新颖性与流畅度区分开来；强模型通过不同配置获得相似总分，有用计划仍可能视觉陈旧；跨模态接地研究表明，文本级VCI排序在忠实渲染和盲选图像偏好判断中基本保持，支持Ekphrasis作为超越散文质量的视觉构思度量。

Conclusion: Ekphrasis基准有效衡量了超越文本质量的视觉构思能力，证明VCI是独立于语言流畅度的重要维度，且文本层面的视觉创意能在后续图像生成环节得到体现。

Abstract: Current evaluations do not isolate whether text-only language models can originate visual concepts before image generation. Fluent visual prose can hide visual-plan failures: an answer may appear creative while repeating familiar visual clichés or failing to specify a renderable scene. We define Visual Creative Ideation (VCI) as the ability to produce textual visual plans that are useful, expressive, and population-novel, and introduce Ekphrasis, a 400-task benchmark spanning Abstraction, Combination, Transformation, and Adaptation. Ekphrasis scores anonymized pairwise comparisons with dimension-specific checklists, aggregates preferences with Bradley-Terry models, and uses Typed Idea Graphs to convert task-specific population clichés into novelty references. Across 14 language models, VCI separates usefulness, expressiveness, and novelty rather than reducing to fluency: strong models achieve similar overall scores through different profiles, and useful plans can remain visually clichéd. A cross-modal grounding study further shows that text-level VCI ordering largely survives faithful rendering and blind image-level preference judgment, supporting Ekphrasis as a measure of visual ideation beyond prose quality.

</details>


### [108] [PHASE-Tree: Modeling Character-State Evolution in Long-Horizon Role-Playing Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06975)
*Bo Tang,Jianan Yang,Junyi Zhu,Yiquan Wu,Rui Zhao,Zhengyu Yang,Yang Zhang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li,Jiajun Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PHASE-Tree框架和LongEvoRoleBench基准，解决长周期角色扮演中角色状态更新与评估难题，显著提升多场景下的角色一致性与演化生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长周期角色扮演研究存在两大缺陷：一是表征通常为静态档案，无法在不破坏未改变特征的情况下进行局部更新；二是基准测试主要关注人设保持和记忆召回，而非模型是否能从角色当前演化状态出发进行对话。

Method: 1. 构建PHASE-Tree：一种多时间尺度角色状态树，包含不可变的身份根节点及可变的人设、会话和时刻层，支持针对特定字段的局部更新（帧内或跨剧集）。2. 引入LongEvoRoleBench：结合四个长对话语料库（用于跨剧集演化）和四个短对话语料库（用于场景内状态追踪），采用统一的下一句预测协议进行评估。

Result: 在长对话核心任务中，文本版PHASE-Tree在12个数据集-指标组合中的11个上排名第一，优于内部变体和所有外部基线。相比基线，角色级、语义和嵌入分数分别提升19.7%、12.4%和15.1%。人类盲评（200条回复）与GPT-4.1自动评分相关性高（Pearson r=0.65），且在描述性子集上整体差异为+0.20。该优势在不同LLM裁判和生成骨干网络中均保持一致。

Conclusion: PHASE-Tree通过结构化状态管理有效解决了长周期角色演化的稳定性问题，LongEvoRoleBench提供了更真实的演化状态评估标准，两者共同推动了长程角色扮演的性能提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon role-playing demands that characters remain recognizable as they evolve with the narrative. Yet existing work falls short on two fronts: representations are typically static profiles that cannot be updated locally without destabilizing unchanged traits, and benchmarks mainly test persona preservation and memory recall rather than whether a model speaks from a character's currently evolved state. We address both. PHASE-Tree is a multi-timescale character-state tree with an immutable identity root and mutable persona, session, and moment layers, making each mutable field an addressable target for localized within- and cross-episode updates. It conditions generation through explicit textual provision or implicit parametric adaptation. To measure evolved-state generation, we introduce LongEvoRoleBench, which pairs four long-dialogue corpora for cross-episode evolution with four short-dialogue corpora as within-scene state-tracking checks, under a unified next-utterance protocol. On the long-dialogue core, textual PHASE-Tree ranks first in 11 of 12 dataset-metric cells against internal variants and all 12 cells against external textual baselines, improving character-level, semantic, and embedding scores by 19.7%, 12.4%, and 15.1% respectively. In a blinded 200-response study, human ratings correlate with the GPT-4.1 judge (Pearson r= 0.65); on descriptive n= 10 PT and NR prompt subsets, the Overall difference is +0.20. The long-dialogue Sem advantage persists across LLM judges and generation backbones.

</details>


### [109] [Confirming Our Biases? Evaluating the Capabilities, Risks, and Societal Impact of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06977)
*Mudar Adas,Polina Tsvilodub,Michael Franke,Martin V. Butz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）对提示框架的敏感性，发现LLM会系统性调整回答以迎合提示中的立场，甚至在事实性领域也是如此，表明提示框架的影响力可能超过事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在调查LLM在多大程度上会强化用户在提示中表达的偏见，并厘清隐性框架效应与显式提示操纵之间的界限。

Method: 使用涵盖10个主题（包括观点型和事实型领域）的160个独特提示，系统性地变化提示策略、支持/挑战指令、提示极性、用户信念及主题领域，对六个LLM进行评估。

Result: LLM的系统性回应会适应并符合提示框架，即使在事实性语境下也是如此；结果划定了LLM可被操纵的程度和边界，显示其能强化细微的用户偏见，且在应保持稳定事实的回答领域中易受显式提示操纵。

Conclusion: 提示框架可以超越事实一致性，影响模型回应；LLM不仅容易受到显式提示操纵，还可能强化用户的隐性偏见，这揭示了LLM在保持客观性和抗操纵能力方面的局限性。

Abstract: It is well established that large language models (LLMs) are sensitive to prompt framing, reflecting patterns in their training data or prior prompts. In this study, we investigate the extent to which LLMs reinforce users biases expressed in the prompts and examine the boundary between implicit framing effects and explicit prompt manipulation. Specifically, we evaluate how susceptible LLMs are to direct and suggestive prompts that encourage models to support or challenge particular positions.
  We evaluate six LLMs using 160 distinct prompts spanning ten topics across opinion-based and factual domains. The prompts systematically vary in prompting strategy, support versus challenge instructions, prompt polarity, users' expressed beliefs, and topic domain, spanning both opinion-based and factual questions. Our results show that LLMs systematically adapt their responses to align with prompt framing, even in factual contexts. This suggests that prompt framing can outweigh factual consistency in model responses. Overall, our findings delineate the extent and boundaries of LLM manipulability. Furthermore, the results imply that LLMs can reinforce subtle user biases and are susceptible to explicit prompt manipulation even in domains where responses should remain factually stable.

</details>


### [110] [Does More Retrieved Evidence Help Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Diffusion Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07006)
*Jiankun Wang,Yisen Gao,Ziwei Zhang,Xingcheng Fu,Jiaxin Bai,Chen Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对视觉RAG中“无条件传入所有检索证据”的假设，研究发现这会导致扩散语言模型（DLMs）因语义冲突降低准确率。提出无训练的熵基候选过滤器（ECF），通过多粒度证据单元和置信度评估进行选择性证据准入，在多个基准上显著提升了视觉问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉检索增强生成（RAG）常假设应将所有检索到的证据传递给生成器以扩大覆盖范围，但本文指出该假设在扩散语言模型（DLMs）中不成立，因为无条件传入所有证据会因语义冲突导致答案准确性下降。

Method: 提出熵基候选过滤器（ECF），一种无需训练的证据准入框架。该方法构建多粒度证据单元以减少无关内容，并利用空白控制块置信度和检索排名来识别有益证据，从而决定哪些候选证据进入最终上下文，实现选择性证据准入而非无条件扩展。

Result: 在三个多模态DLMs和五个视觉QA基准测试中，ECF相比最强的固定top-k输入方法平均提升2.62个百分点的准确率；与LLaDA2.0-Uni相比，在各数据集的最佳无训练结果基础上平均提升2.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 更广泛的检索确实有利于视觉DLM-RAG，但关键在于通过选择性证据准入而非无条件证据扩展来实现这一优势。代码已公开。

Abstract: Visual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) commonly expands the retrieved evidence set to improve answer-page coverage, implicitly assuming that all available evidence should be passed to the generator. We show that this assumption does not hold for diffusion language models (DLMs): retrieving more pages increases answer-page recall, whereas unconditionally passing all retrieved pages to the generator often reduces answer accuracy, primarily because of semantic conflict. A latent-source analysis explains this mismatch through source-coherence loss in parallel denoising, where position-wise proposals can combine incompatible visual sources into unsupported answers. We further find that such interference is already visible in the first-step answer-block distribution, making it possible to assess evidence before decoding. To preserve retrieval coverage while limiting harmful visual exposure, we propose the Entropy-Based Candidate Filter (ECF), a training-free evidence-admission framework. To reduce irrelevant content within individual candidates, ECF constructs multi-granularity evidence units; to identify beneficial additional evidence, it uses blank-controlled block confidence and retrieval rank to determine whether and which candidate should enter the final context. Across three multimodal DLMs and five visual QA benchmarks, ECF improves answer accuracy by 2.62 percentage points on average over the strongest fixed top-$k$ input and, with LLaDA2.0-Uni, by 2.37 percentage points on average over the best competing training-free result for each dataset. These results show that broader retrieval benefits visual DLM-RAG through selective evidence admission rather than unconditional evidence expansion. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/wjkuser/ECF.

</details>


### [111] [An Agentic Hybrid Top-Down and Bottom-Up Approach to Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07023)
*Emma Jouffroy,Warren Jouanneau,Marc Palyart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM和Wikidata知识图谱的混合生成流水线，通过代理反思模式解决多语言非标准化技能声明的组织难题，实现可扩展、可解释且自修复的技能知识图谱构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人力资源平台面临成千上万非标准化、多语言技能声明组织的挑战，这直接影响人才匹配等下游任务的准确性。现有自上而下或自下而上的方法存在局限性，无法有效应对快速演变且噪声较多的技能描述。

Method: 构建了一个混合知识图谱生成流水线：1) 将大语言模型（LLM）锚定到Wikidata多语言知识图谱；2) 采用代理反思（agentic reflexion）模式合成新概念及其元数据；3) 执行五个阶段：实体对齐、多语言规范化、主动策展、去重以及未映射概念的迭代恢复；4) 对已识别概念锚定稳定KG实体，对未识别技能动态创建新节点和关系元数据。

Result: 系统能够自主适应五种欧洲语言中快速演变且带有噪声的技能提及，成功从非结构化、噪声文本中生成全面的技能知识图谱，并从中导出结构化分类法。该方法具有高度可扩展性、可解释性和自修复能力。

Conclusion: 该混合流水线提供了一种高效、灵活且鲁棒的解决方案，用于处理HR领域复杂的多语言技能数据，为精准人才匹配奠定了坚实的知识基础。

Abstract: Organizing thousands of unstandardized, multilingual expertise declarations is a persistent challenge for Human Resources (HR) platforms, directly impacting downstream tasks like accurate talent matching. To address this, we propose a hybrid knowledge graph generation pipeline that grounds a Large Language Model (LLM) in the Wikidata multilingual Knowledge Graph (KG) while employing an agentic reflexion pattern to synthesize emerging concepts and their associated metadata. Unlike rigid top-down methods or fragmented bottom-up approaches, our system anchors recognized concepts to stable Knowledge Graph entities while dynamically creating new nodes and relational metadata for unrecognized skills. Executed across five stages, entity reconciliation, multilingual canonicalization, active curation, deduplication, and the iterative recovery of unmapped concepts, the system autonomously adapts to rapidly evolving, noisy skill mentions across five European languages. Ultimately, this pipeline provides a highly scalable, explicable, and self-healing framework for generating a comprehensive skills knowledge graph, from which a structured taxonomy is derived, using unstructured, noisy text.

</details>


### [112] [HNR-DAC: Hard-Negative Reranking and Distribution-Aligned Classification for Scientific Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07204)
*Zhenchao Wang,Xin Chen,Luoxi Zhang,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HNR-DAC两阶段框架，通过硬负样本重排和分布对齐分类解决科学声明验证中的证据混淆与推理偏差问题，在NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2中取得优异排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学声明验证面临两大挑战：一是论文内部的干扰项常与真实证据相似；二是训练时使用的黄金证据与推理时检索到的证据存在分布差异，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出HNR-DAC两阶段框架：1) 硬负样本重排（HNR）：利用基础重排器对非黄金段落评分，量化证据混淆度，并将黄金证据与最混淆候选者进行对比学习；2) 分布对齐分类（DAC）：使用冻结的HNR生成的Top-1段落作为训练输入，以模拟推理时的实际数据分布，同时输出Top-3段落标识作为证据结果。

Result: 最终配置在测试集上获得97.21% Hit@3、95.79% Macro-F1、94.47% Joint@3及95.13%平均分；官方提交版本排名第3，Macro-F1达93.05%，Joint@3为70.16%，平均分81.61%。

Conclusion: HNR-DAC框架通过针对性地处理训练与推理阶段的分布不一致性及证据混淆问题，显著提升了科学声明验证的性能，并在权威竞赛中证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Scientific claim verification over a cited paper requires predicting the claim--paper relation and identifying the paragraphs that justify that prediction. This setting poses two linked challenges: within-paper distractors often resemble genuine evidence, while a classifier trained on gold evidence must operate on retrieved evidence at inference. We present HNR-DAC, a two-stage framework that trains each stage on the cases it will actually encounter. Hard-Negative Reranking (HNR) quantifies evidence confusability using a base reranker's scores on non-gold paragraphs and contrasts gold evidence against the most confusable candidates. Distribution-Aligned Classification (DAC) trains on the Top-1 paragraph produced by the same frozen HNR used to construct inference inputs, while HNR's Top-3 paragraph identifiers provide the evidence output. On the NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2, the final configuration obtains 97.21% Hit@3, 95.79% Macro-F1, 94.47% Joint@3, and an average score of 95.13%. The corresponding submission ranks third on the official Track 2 leaderboard while achieving the highest overall Macro-F1 of 93.05%, alongside 70.16% Joint@3 and an average score of 81.61%.

</details>


### [113] [Measuring Concept Content in Text from LLM Activations: ESG Evidence from Concept Vectors and Linear Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07208)
*Luc Hazenoot,Zhaochun Ren,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用冻结的大语言模型（LLM）内部激活状态来衡量文本概念内容，无需任务特定微调。通过线性探测和递归特征机（RFM）提取指标，在ESG金融文本数据集上验证，发现线性探测精度接近微调分类器且优于模型自身回答，证明激活状态携带了响应中未报告的深层概念信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本概念度量方法（如词频、主题比例、嵌入相似度）仅关注表面词汇，忽略了读者形成的判断。同时，LLM内部知识与外部表达之间存在差距，旨在探索是否能用冻结LLM的内部激活替代微调来测量概念内容，并确定最佳提取方法。

Method: 使用递归特征机（RFM）算法和线性探测从冻结的通用LLM中提取概念测量指标。将这些方法与嵌入基线、表面基线以及模型自身的回答进行对比。实验基于人类标注的环境、社会和治理（ESG）金融文本数据集进行。

Result: 最佳线性探测方法的准确率仅比微调领域分类器低0.6个百分点，且在12次比较中有11次优于模型自身的回答。简单的线性探测 consistently 优于RFM概念向量。RFM提供了反映概念强度的连续分数，而不仅仅是分类结果。

Conclusion: LLM的内部激活状态确实包含了其外部响应中未报告的概念内容，可作为无需微调的高效概念测量手段。线性探测是提取此类信息的有效方法，而RFM则能提供更细粒度的连续评分，尽管后者需要分级标签进行进一步验证。

Abstract: Existing measures of how much a text is about a concept read the surface of the text: dictionary word shares, topic proportions, embedding similarities. They score the words a text uses, not the judgment a reader forms about it. Recent work has shown that a gap exists in what Large Language Models (LLMs) know internally versus what they express in their response. This paper asks whether that internal knowledge, read by monitoring the activations of frozen, out-of-the-box LLMs, can stand in for task-specific fine-tuning when measuring concept content, and which extraction method reads it best. We extract such measures via the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm and via linear probing, and compare these against an embedding baseline, surface baselines, and the same model's own answer to the question. We demonstrate the approach on financial text, a domain studied extensively and served by established annotated resources, using a human-annotated Environmental, Social and Governance (ESG) dataset. The best linear probe comes within 0.6 percentage points of a fine-tuned domain classifier's accuracy without any task-specific fine-tuning, and outscores the same model's own answer to the question in eleven of twelve comparisons, so the activations carry concept content the response does not report. The simple probe consistently beats the RFM concept vectors, which in turn provide what classification alone does not: a continuous score intended to reflect how strongly a concept is present in a text, whose validation awaits graded labels.

</details>


### [114] [From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07213)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yuanxi Che,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文解决了文本到SQL领域中“结晶问题”，即如何评估系统保留已验证修复片段对后续查询的实际价值。通过控制变量实验发现，存储已验证的修正查询能显著提升未见过的同类数据库查询的首次尝试准确率（提升4.34个百分点），且数据库特异性内容是主要贡献因素，而非复杂的检索器或格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时扩展方法虽然能纠正困难的文本到SQL查询，但其额外计算通常在生成答案后被丢弃。尽管系统日益倾向于保留已验证的修复片段，但现有评估仅报告端到端分数，无法区分记忆回放对重复问题的帮助与对未见问题的帮助，也无法识别具体是哪种记忆选择产生了效果。因此，需要一种方法来衡量这种“未来价值”。

Method: 提出“结晶问题”概念，并设计受控评估框架：固定单次求解器，每次仅改变一个记忆选择变量。分别测量回放效果、跨问题保留效果以及在保持同一数据库情况下的迁移效果。在BIRD数据集上进行实验，分析不同记忆策略（如存储已验证修正查询、数据库特异性内容等）对模型性能的影响。

Result: 1. 在BIRD数据集上，存储已验证的修正查询使未见问题的首次尝试准确率提高了4.34个百分点。2. 这一增益占到了按需修复所提供准确率潜力的44.4%。3. 受控干预表明，数据库特异性内容是主要的有效成分。4. 可靠的验证机制和更广泛的检索覆盖率带来了显著增益，而更丰富的格式和复杂的检索器并未带来额外收益。

Conclusion: 记忆机制在文本到SQL任务中具有显著的未来价值，特别是存储经过验证的、具有数据库特异性的修正查询能有效提升模型对未见数据的泛化能力。简单的可靠验证和广泛覆盖比复杂的检索策略更有效。研究提供了开源代码和复现材料以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can correct difficult text-to-SQL queries, but the extra computation is normally discarded after each answer. Systems increasingly retain verified repair episodes, yet evaluations still report one end-to-end score. It cannot distinguish replay on recurring questions from help on unseen questions, or identify the responsible memory choice. We call measuring this future value the crystallization problem. Our controlled evaluation holds the single-shot solver fixed and varies one memory choice at a time. We separately measure replay, cross-question retention, and held-out same-database transfer. On BIRD, storing verified corrected queries improves held-out first-attempt accuracy by 4.34 percentage points. This gain captures 44.4% of the accuracy headroom provided by on-demand repair on the same questions. Controlled interventions identify database-specific content as the main operating ingredient. Reliable verification and broader retrieval coverage yield supported gains; richer formats and elaborate retrievers do not. Open-source code, evaluation artifacts, and reproduction instructions are available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/text-to-sql-memory-crystallization.

</details>


### [115] [Skaling: Chinchilla's Exponents Meet Kaplan's Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07222)
*Mathurin Videau,Badr Youbi-Idrissi,David Lopez-Paz,Kartik Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“Skaling law”，通过引入单一交互指数耦合模型容量与数据，修正了传统神经缩放定律在数据稀缺和过度训练极端情况下的预测偏差，显著降低误差并减少计算资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经缩放定律基于模型大小和训练数据独立影响损失的假设，导致在数据稀缺和过度训练的极端情况下系统性地低估或高估损失，无法准确预测性能。

Method: 提出广义函数形式“Skaling law”，将模型容量和数据通过一个单一的交互指数进行耦合；结合仅限低计算量范围的稀疏网格策略，实现全网格外推。

Result: 该扩展方法在插值和外推 regimes 中将平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）降低了1.5-3倍；配合稀疏网格策略，使用比均匀扫描少约10倍的计算量即可实现准确的全网格外推。

Conclusion: Skaling law 通过从小规模实验可靠预测性能，为下一代模型训练中的计算预算分配提供了更稳健且资源高效的框架。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws are foundational for language model development, yet standard formulations systematically under- and overestimate loss at data-scarce and overtraining extremes. This failure originates in the underlying assumption that model size and training data impact the loss independently. To address this, we introduce the Skaling law, a generalized functional form that couples model capacity and data through a single interaction exponent. This simple extension reduces the Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) by 1.5-3x across both interpolation and extrapolation regimes. When paired with a sparse grid strategy restricted to low-compute regimes, the Skaling law achieves accurate full-grid extrapolation using approximately 10x less compute than uniform sweeps. By enabling reliable performance prediction from small-scale experiments, the Skaling law provides a more robust and resource-efficient framework for allocating compute budgets in next-generation model training.

</details>


### [116] [Stoicheia: Character-Level Masked Diffusion for Ancient Greek Textual Restoration, Parsing, and Metrical Scansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07249)
*Eric Cullhed,Albin Thörn Cleland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Stoicheia 是一个专为古希腊语设计的 4.05 亿参数字符级掩码扩散编码器。它通过五个独立可掩码的平面（字母、边界、变音符号、大小写和标点）处理文本，能够无需重新分词即可同时修复残缺、重新分段、添加重音和标点。在包含 3.8 亿词的开源语料库上预训练后，其在 Ithaca 测试集上将字符错误率从之前的 SOTA 降至 15.5，Top-1 准确率提升至 74.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决古希腊语文本处理中的多个任务（如修复残缺铭文、语法标注、重音添加等），通常依赖于特定任务的重新分词或单独模型。研究希望开发一个统一的骨干网络，能够在一个框架内恢复文本缺失部分、重新分割、添加重音和标点，从而提高效率并统一处理流程。

Method: 提出 Stoicheia 模型，这是一个 4.05 亿参数的字符级掩码扩散编码器。其输入被分解为五个对齐且独立可掩码的平面：字母、单词和句子边界、变音符号、大小写和标点。使用单一骨干网络实现多任务处理。在 3.8 亿词的开源、修订锁定语料库上进行预训练，并发布 11 个检查点（10 个旋转去污染折叠和一个无文献文本暴露的折叠），以确保评估的严谨性。

Result: 1. **多任务性能提升**：在铭文重建中降低 5.6 CER；在依存句法分析中提高 12.9 LAS；在长音标记与格律扫描中提高 6.0 点平衡准确率。
2. **Ithaca 基准测试**：在严格的评分标准下，Stoicheia 将字符错误率从 Ithaca (24.6) 及其后继者 Aeneas-framework (23.5) 显著降低至 15.5。
3. **准确率突破**：Top-1 准确率从 63.0% 和 64.0% 提升至 74.5%，优于现有最先进系统。

Conclusion: Stoicheia 证明了字符级扩散预训练在古希腊语处理中的有效性。通过统一的掩码扩散架构，该模型能够在不依赖任务特定重新分词的情况下，显著提升文本修复、语法分析和正字法校正的性能，确立了新的状态-of-the-art 基准。

Abstract: We introduce Stoicheia, a 405M-parameter character-level masked-diffusion encoder for Ancient Greek whose input factors into five aligned, independently maskable planes: letters, word and sentence boundaries, diacritics, capitalization, and punctuation. A single backbone can therefore restore lacunae, re-segment, accentuate, and punctuate unspaced text without task-specific retokenization. We pretrain it on an open, revision-pinned corpus of 380M words and release eleven checkpoints: ten rotated, decontaminated folds, guaranteeing that for any given literary passage at least one released model has never seen its text, and one with no exposure to documentary texts. Three experiments - reconstruction of damaged inscriptions and papyri, morphosyntactic tagging and dependency parsing, and macronization with metrical scansion - each carry a matched random-initialization control, isolating what character-level diffusion pretraining contributes: 5.6 CER points on inscription reconstruction, 12.9 LAS on parsing, and 6.0 points of balanced accuracy on macronization. On Ithaca's own test split, with identical frozen samples and strict scoring, Stoicheia reduces character error relative to both prior state-of-the-art systems, from 24.6 (Ithaca) and 23.5 (its 2025 Aeneas-framework successor) to 15.5, and raises top-1 accuracy from 63.0 and 64.0 to 74.5.

</details>


### [117] [Why Knowing Both Hops Is Not Enough: Understanding Two-Hop Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07261)
*Zili Zhang,Yilin Wang,Heng Wang,Herun Wan,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在处理简单两跳查询时存在“存储正确但组合失败”的现象。通过从零训练Transformer并进行机制分析，揭示了成功泛化依赖于跨上下文的中间表示一致性，而失败源于层间不匹配（下层构建表示，上层直接映射输出）。据此提出的循环式训练策略显著提升了分布外两跳查询的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型语言模型（LLMs）为何能在复杂多跳问题上表现良好，却在简单的两跳查询上出现令人困惑的失败：即模型能正确存储单个跳跃步骤，却无法将它们结合起来。旨在揭示这一现象的内部机制。

Method: 在受控符号环境中从零开始训练Transformer；进行机制分析以解释不同的泛化行为；提出一种循环式训练策略（recurrent-style training strategy），使Transformer能够跨输入形式重用推理电路。

Result: 1. 发现两跳泛化模式：当第二跳遵循训练分布时模型可靠泛化，偏离时则总是失败。2. 机制解释：成功时由一致中间表示驱动；失败时因层间不匹配——下层正确构建中间表示，但上层主要学习从原子事实到输出的映射而非推理。3. 新策略有效提高了分布外两跳查询的泛化性能。

Conclusion: Transformer在两跳任务中的泛化能力受限于层间功能分工的不匹配。通过引入循环式训练策略，强制模型重用推理电路而非仅记忆映射，可以有效解决分布外泛化失败的问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve complex multi-hop problems yet exhibit puzzling failures on simple two-hop queries: although a model may correctly store each individual hop, it often fails to combine them. To understand the internal mechanisms of this phenomenon, we train transformers from scratch in a controlled symbolic environment. Our experiments reveal a pattern in two-hop generalization: models generalize reliably when the second hop follows the training distribution, but always fail when it deviates.
  Through mechanistic analysis, we provide a complete explanation for these distinct generalization behaviors: in settings where models generalize successfully, performance is driven by the emergence of consistent intermediate representations for the same entities across contexts, whereas failures on settings where the second hop is out-of-distribution arise from a mismatch across layers: lower layers correctly construct these intermediate representations, but upper layers, while trained on corresponding atomic facts, primarily learn to map them to outputs rather than to reason over them.
  Driven by this insight, we propose a recurrent-style training strategy, which enables transformers to reuse their reasoning circuitry across input forms and substantially improves generalization on out-of-distribution two-hop queries.

</details>


### [118] [Gaze Behavior in Visual World Experiments Can be Modeled With Off-the-shelf Language-Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07282)
*Rahul Murali Shankar,Titus von der Malsburg,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合CLIP多模态双编码器与双模态归因方法的新模型，用于预测视觉世界实验中的眼动行为。该模型无需微调或生成式架构，即可复现人类语言处理中的预测性加工现象，填补了多模态心理语言学研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经语言模型在计算心理语言学中的应用主要集中于单一模态（书面或口语），而忽视了同时包含视觉和语言输入的多模态实验范式（如视觉世界研究）。

Method: 结合CLIP家族的多模态双编码器模型与双模态归因方法，构建新框架以预测视觉世界研究中的眼动行为。

Result: 该方法能够稳健地复现一项经典的英语视觉世界研究结果，展示了人类预测性加工的特征；且无需生成式架构、无需针对任务进行微调，尽管未专门为此任务训练。

Conclusion: 即使未经特定任务训练，基于CLIP的多模态双编码器结合归因分析也能有效模拟人类在多模态环境下的语言处理过程，为理解神经语言模型与人类认知之间的关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: The recent advances in neural language models have also spurred much work in computational psycholinguistics, asking whether neural LMs are also promising models of human language processing. However, work has been overwhelmingly focused on the unimodal case of written or spoken language. In contrast, multimodal experimental paradigms, like visual world studies that present participants with both visual and linguistic input simultaneously, have been neglected. In this paper, we present a novel approach that predicts gaze behavior in visual world studies. It does so by combining a simple multi-modal bi-encoder model of the CLIP family with a bimodal attribution method. We demonstrate the ability of this approach to robustly replicate the results of a seminal English visual world study which shows hu- man predictive processing. Remarkably, it does so without a generative architecture and without the need for fine-tuning, despite not being trained for this task.

</details>


### [119] [Grammar Engineering Meets LLMs: Development of Cantonese and Irish ParGram Treebanks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07283)
*Chit-Fung Lam,Elaine Uí Dhonnchadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在平行语法项目中开发了粤语和爱尔兰语树库，并评估了多语言大语言模型（LLM）在该工程中的辅助潜力。结果显示LLM翻译效果不佳，句法生成能力有限，虽能提供部分参考分析，但仍需依赖专家驱动的分析与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行语法项目需要兼顾跨语言一致性与特定语言属性，这对语言学形式化和计算实现提出了高要求；同时探索如何利用新兴的多语言LLM来支持这一复杂的语法工程过程。

Method: 1. 在Parallel Grammar (ParGram) 项目中开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库，保持抽象功能层面的语言平行性。2. 使用OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b模型进行粤语-爱尔兰语翻译实验及形式化句法结构生成任务，测试其在不同提示语言下的表现。

Result: 1. 翻译性能总体不满意，且不受提示语言影响。2. 句法结构生成能产生部分有意义的输出，但在需要跨语言抽象的任务上表现糟糕。3. LLM生成的输出具有参考价值，能提供替代分析思路及部分捕捉谓词-论元关系。

Conclusion: LLMs在协作式语法工程中既有潜力也有局限性，不能完全替代人工，专家驱动的分析与验证依然至关重要。

Abstract: Grammar engineering requires expertise in linguistic formalism and computational implementation, especially in parallel grammar projects that balance cross-linguistic consistency with language-specific properties. This paper presents the development of Cantonese and Irish treebanks within the Parallel Grammar (ParGram) Project, where linguistic parallelism is maintained at an abstract functional level. We also investigate the methodological potential and limitations of using multilingual LLMs to support grammar engineering, focusing on Cantonese-Irish translation and the generation of formal syntactic structures using OpenAI's gpt-oss-120b model. The results show that translation performance was generally unsatisfactory and unaffected by prompt language. For syntactic structure generation, the model produced some structurally meaningful outputs, but performed poorly on tasks requiring cross-linguistic abstraction. Nonetheless, LLM-generated outputs may still offer some reference value by suggesting alternative analyses and (partially) capturing predicate-argument relations. Overall, our findings highlight both the potential and limitations of using LLMs in collaborative grammar engineering, while underscoring the continued importance of expert-driven analysis and verification.

</details>


### [120] [Natural Language Processing Psychometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07316)
*Edoardo Sebastiano De Duro,Emma Franchino,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出“NLP心理测量学”框架，利用LLM生成的合成数据（认知数字阴影）进行心理预测。通过结合情感特征、句法语义网络及人口统计学变量构建随机森林模型，实现了对生活满意度、抑郁、焦虑和压力的高精度预测，并验证了模型在未见文本上的泛化能力及可解释性，强调合成数据需配合人类验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP模型在预测心理健康结果时，往往缺乏对测量内容（如语境知识、情感内容或句法结构）的明确界定，且难以提供可解释的语言学证据。

Method: 1. 让9种大语言模型（LLMs）基于受控的人格画像完成带有文字解释的心理测量问卷；2. 提取情感概况和句法-语义结构形成“ textual forma mentis networks”；3. 将这些特征与人格及社会人口学变量结合，使用消融随机森林回归器进行分析；4. 利用SHAP值识别驱动性能的关键特征及其方向。

Result: 1. 全量RF模型解释了高达70.8%的生活满意度方差，55.7%的抑郁方差，以及DASS-21中68.5%的抑郁、76.0%的焦虑和72.4%的压力方差；2. 社会人口学变量仅对生活满意度有显著解释力（主要预测因子为情感和收入），而神经质和网络拓扑主导了抑郁和焦虑预测；3. 无需重新训练，模型即可区分日记中的高低分人格（相关系数最高达0.91），并仅凭网络和情感特征以68%的准确率分类真实转录本中的临床与控制参与者。

Conclusion: 合成数据（LLM人格）能揭示模型偏差、恢复符合临床反刍的模式，并支持无需匹配问卷的心理预测，但不能替代人类验证。NLP心理测量学通过可解释AI和网络/情感特征，使这些区分变得明确、可测量和可测试。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) models predicting mental health outcomes rarely specify what they measure: contextual knowledge, emotional content, or syntactic structure. NLP Psychometrics treats psychological prediction from text as a psychometric problem, linking scores to interpretable linguistic evidence and testing beyond the training text format. Nine LLMs, conditioned on controlled personas (cognitive digital shadows), completed psychometric questionnaires with textual explanations per item. We extracted emotional profiles and syntactic-semantic structure via textual forma mentis networks, combined with personality and sociodemographic variables in ablated random forest (RF) regressors, using SHAP to identify which features drove performance and in which direction. Full RF models explained up to 70.8% of variance in life satisfaction (SWLS), 55.7% in depression (PHQ-9), and, for DASS-21, 68.5% depression, 76.0% anxiety, 72.4% stress. Sociodemographics alone explained no meaningful variance in depression, anxiety, or stress, but did so for life satisfaction, where emotion features and income were the strongest predictors; neuroticism and network topology instead dominated depression and anxiety, reversing direction between them. Without retraining, RF models separated diaries from low- and high-score personas ($r$ up to 0.91) and, using only network/emotion features, classified clinical from control participants in real transcripts with up to 68% accuracy. These results show the promise and limits of synthetic data: LLM personas can expose model biases, recover patterns consistent with clinical rumination, and support psychometric prediction from human text without a matched questionnaire, but cannot substitute for human validation. NLP Psychometrics makes these distinctions explicit, measurable, and testable through interpretable AI and network/emotional features.

</details>


### [121] [Zero Gap Is Not Restoration: Stratified Per-Question Probability Evaluation and Step-wise Mitigation of Benchmark Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07341)
*Ruijie Hou,Yueyang Jiao,Zhao Wang,Yingming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对数据污染评估指标G-AP的缺陷，提出新指标SA-PPG以更准确衡量模型去污后的真实能力；同时提出RailCap方法，通过在生成过程中动态抑制贪婪轨迹来有效缓解污染，实验表明其优于现有策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准测试数据不可避免地泄露到预训练语料中，导致模型记忆并虚高评估分数。现有的“污染缓解评估”旨在通过干预解码过程来抑制记忆并恢复模型的真正能力，但其主流指标G-AP存在严重缺陷：离散的正确/错误读数无法表征每道题的性能、平均后再相减会导致过度与不足抑制相互抵消、以及均匀加权容易诱导策略将解题概率推向干净模型的高频值，从而无法真实反映模型能力。

Method: 1. 提出新评估指标SA-PPG（分层聚合每题概率差）：通过采样估计每道题的解题概率，与干净模型对比得出每题的概率差，并根据干净模型的解题概率进行分组聚合。
2. 提出新方法RailCap：不同于先估计污染位置再操作的传统策略，RailCap在生成过程中判断污染：当样本回退到贪婪轨迹时，将下一个轨迹令牌限制为次优选项（runner-up），累积抑制直到响应分布充分分散。

Result: 在多个受污染的模型和基准测试上，SA-PPG指标显示 prior strategies（先前策略）对模型能力的恢复被显著高估，而RailCap取得了最低的SA-PPG得分，表明其去污效果最佳。

Conclusion: G-AP指标存在根本性缺陷，不能准确评估污染缓解效果。SA-PPG提供了更严谨的评估框架，而RailCap作为一种无需预先估计污染位置的新型去污策略，能更有效地恢复模型在污染数据上的真实泛化能力。

Abstract: Test data from public benchmarks inevitably leaks into pretraining corpora, inflating evaluation scores once memorized. \textbf{Contamination mitigation evaluation} intervenes in the decoding process to suppress memorization and restore a contaminated model's genuine capability, but its prevailing metric, the \textbf{G-AP} (\textbf{G}ap of \textbf{A}ggregate \textbf{P}erformance), is flawed. Discrete correct/incorrect readouts cannot characterize per-question performance, averaging before differencing lets over- and under-suppression cancel out, and uniform per-question weighting invites strategies to push solve probabilities onto the clean model's high-frequency values. We propose \textbf{SA-PPG} (\textbf{S}tratified \textbf{A}ggregate of \textbf{P}er-question \textbf{P}robability \textbf{G}aps): estimate each question's solve probability by sampling, difference it against the clean model per question, and aggregate within groups defined by the clean model's solve probability. Existing mitigation strategies first estimate where contamination lies and then operate on the estimate, so they are only as correct as the estimate. \textbf{RailCap} instead judges contamination during generation: whenever a sample falls back onto the greedy trajectory, the next trajectory token is capped to the runner-up, accumulating suppression until the response distribution becomes sufficiently dispersed. Across multiple contaminated models and benchmarks, SA-PPG reveals that prior strategies' restoration is substantially overestimated, while RailCap attains the lowest SA-PPG.

</details>


### [122] [LitTraceQA: A Benchmark for Multi-Stage Grounding and Verification in Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07370)
*Xuye Liu,Yimu Wang,Peng Shi,Bo Xue,Xiangrui Ke,Songcheng Cai,Kath Choi,Di Wu,Freda Shi,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LitTraceQA，一个面向科学文献的问答基准测试。该基准要求系统不仅能回答问题，还需提供相关的论文ID、具体证据位置（如表格、图表、文本等）以及多种格式的答案，旨在评估科学问答系统的可验证性和忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型和检索增强生成系统在利用科学文献时，往往只注重生成的流畅性，而缺乏对证据来源的准确定位和对答案忠实度的保证。因此，需要一种能够评估系统识别相关论文、定位具体证据并生成基于证据回答能力的可靠标准。

Method: 构建了LitTraceQA基准测试，包含开发集（55个示例）和大规模最终注释集合（4,978个唯一问题记录，覆盖4,859篇唯一黄金论文）。系统需从元数据池中返回三个关联输出：规范化的论文标识符、支持证据的位置（涵盖表格、图表、文本片段、公式/算法及引用上下文），以及自由文本、多项选择或结构化表格等多种格式的答案。通过分别评估论文检索、证据 grounding 和答案准确性来进行分析。

Result: 提供了包含公开开发分裂集和大规模注释数据集的完整基准。该基准涵盖了常见的科学阅读证据类型，并为本地验证提供了黄金论文、证据标注和答案。通过对检索、证据定位和答案准确性的独立评估，确立了用于测试产生可验证答案而非无根据摘要的科学QA系统的测试床。

Conclusion: LitTraceQA为科学问答系统提供了一个新的评估框架，强调答案的可验证性和对原始证据的忠实度，推动了从单纯的内容生成向基于证据的可靠推理转变。

Abstract: Scientific literature is increasingly used as a knowledge source for language models, retrieval-augmented generation systems, and research assistants, but answering research questions from papers requires more than fluent generation. A reliable system must identify the relevant papers, locate the concrete evidence that supports the answer, and produce a response that is faithful to that evidence. We present LitTraceQA, a benchmark for literature-grounded question answering over scientific papers. Given a research question and a metadata pool of papers, a system must return three connected outputs: canonical paper identifiers, supporting evidence locations, and answers in one or more requested formats, including free-form text, multiple-choice answers, and structured tables. LitTraceQA targets evidence types common in scientific reading: tables, figures, text spans, equations or algorithms, and citation contexts. The public development split contains 55 examples, including 26 hidden-source single-paper questions and 29 multi-paper questions, and provides gold papers, evidence annotations, and answers for local validation. We also analyze a larger final annotation collection with 4,978 unique-question records over 4,859 unique gold papers. By evaluating paper retrieval, evidence grounding, and answer accuracy separately, LitTraceQA provides a testbed for scientific QA systems that produce verifiable answers rather than unsupported summaries.

</details>


### [123] [An Exploratory Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Rewriting of Moderate-Complexity Financial Sentences for DisCoCat-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07439)
*Brian Llinas,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种大语言模型（LLM）辅助的预处理工作流，通过受控重写技术将中等复杂度的金融情感句子压缩、简化或分解为兼容解析器且电路高效的变体。实验表明，该方法能显著降低量子电路资源消耗（qubit和gate计数减少70%以上），并在GPT-4.1-mini配合特定Prompt策略下，使平均准确率从基线的0.521提升至0.550，验证了LLM辅助重写在提升DisCoCat框架可扩展性方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分布组合范畴（DisCoCat）的量子自然语言处理（QNLP）在金融情感分析中存在实际局限，包括对解析器的敏感性、高昂的模拟成本以及处理长句子的困难。

Method: 构建LLM辅助的预处理工作流，利用受控重写技术对中等复杂度的金融情感句子进行处理；比较不同的提示策略（prompting strategies）、语言模型及过滤配置，并与仅使用低复杂度输入的DisCoCat基线进行对比；在电路层面评估资源消耗，在应用层面评估情感分析准确率。

Result: 最强的压缩变体使平均qubit和gate计数相对原始中等复杂度子集减少了70%以上；GPT-4.1-mini配合Prompt B达到最高平均准确率0.550 ± 0.035，优于基线的0.521 ± 0.050；训练集大小与准确率呈中度负相关（Pearson r=-0.446）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助重写可使部分中等复杂度输入适用于当前的DisCoCat配置，证明了其在缓解QNLP扩展性瓶颈方面的有效性；同时指出提示设计、过滤机制和电路感知预处理是实现更可扩展的基于QNLP的金融情感分析的关键考量因素。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) provides a grammar-aware framework for text modeling, and Distributional Compositional Categorical (DisCoCat) is one of its theoretically grounded formulations. Prior work on financial sentiment analysis has identified practical limitations of DisCoCat, including parser sensitivity, high simulation cost, and difficulty handling longer sentences. We study an LLM-assisted preprocessing workflow that uses controlled rewriting to compress, simplify, or decompose moderate-complexity financial sentiment sentences into parser-compatible, circuit-efficient variants while preserving sentiment-bearing meaning. We compare prompting strategies, language models, and filtering configurations with the low-complexity-only DisCoCat baseline of Stein et al. At the circuit level, the strongest compression variants reduce average qubit and gate counts by more than 70 percent relative to the raw moderate-complexity subset. Across repeated training runs, GPT-4.1-mini with Prompt B achieves the highest observed mean accuracy, $0.550 \pm 0.035$, compared with $0.521 \pm 0.050$ for the baseline. Larger training splits do not necessarily improve downstream performance; across evaluated configurations, training-split size has a moderately negative association with accuracy (Pearson $r=-0.446$). These results provide exploratory evidence that LLM-assisted rewriting can make some moderate-complexity inputs usable within the evaluated DisCoCat configuration, while highlighting prompt design, filtering, and circuit-aware preprocessing as considerations for more scalable QNLP-based financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [124] [CreativeInstruct: Scalably Teaching LLMs to Balance Quality, Creativity, and Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07460)
*Ananya Sahu,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CreativeInstruct方法，通过指令微调让LLM在保持质量的同时注入创意，并引入基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标。实验显示该方法提升了叙事生成的多样性和人类评分的创造性认可度，并增强了强化学习（RL）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽然提升了大语言模型（LLMs）的能力，但通常降低了输出的多样性和创造性，这对需要显式或隐式创造性的任务（如故事生成和强化学习）产生负面影响。

Method: 提出了CreativeInstruct，一种可扩展的指令微调方法，通过学习注入特殊的[StartCreativity]跨度来引导生成偏向创造性，从而平衡基础模型的生成风格与后训练模型的质量。同时，引入了基于图编辑距离的结构多样性度量，以捕捉纯词汇和语义指标遗漏的叙事层面变化。

Result: 在叙事生成任务中，CreativeInstruct在无需推理时调用多个模型的情况下，其多样性匹配或超过了多模型基线和蒸馏变体，且未牺牲质量。人类评估显示，70.3%的情况下标注者认为CreativeInstruct的生成比后训练LLM更具创造性。此外，将GRPO应用于CreativeInstruct检查点相比后训练检查点，在AMC上提升约4%，在MATH上提升约5个百分点。

Conclusion: CreativeInfect有效解决了后训练导致的创造力下降问题，不仅提升了生成内容的多样性和创造性质量，还证明了创造性模型作为强化学习底层的优势。

Abstract: While post-training improves the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), it generally lowers their output diversity and creativity, negatively impacting tasks that explicitly require creativity (e.g., story generation) as well as those that require it implicitly, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL). We instead propose CreativeInstruct, a scalable instruction-tuning method that teaches LLMs to balance creative, base-model-like generations with the quality of post-trained models, by learning to inject special [StartCreativity] spans that bias generation toward creativity. Furthermore, we introduce a structural diversity metric based on graph edit distance, which captures narrative level variation missed by purely lexical and semantic metrics. On narrative generation, CreativeInstruct matches or exceeds the diversity of both multi-model baselines and distilled variants of their outputs, without sacrificing quality or requiring multiple models at inference time. These results are mirrored in our human evaluation, where we find that annotators rate CreativeInstruct generations as more creative than the post-trained LLMs' generations in 70.3% of cases. We also show the benefits of creative models as a substrate for RL: GRPO applied to a CreativeInstruct checkpoint improves by ~4% on AMC and ~5% points on MATH over the same training applied to the post-trained checkpoint.

</details>


### [125] [Mitigating Scoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge via Random Number Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05726)
*Yuma Asato,Kiyoaki Shirai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过检测大语言模型(LLM)在随机数字生成中的潜在数值偏差来缓解“LLM-as-a-Judge”评分偏见的方法，并在多项评估任务中证明其优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM作为文本质量评估器时存在的评分偏见（scoring bias），即无论输入文本上下文如何，LLM倾向于生成特定分数的问题，以超越依赖参考文本的传统自动评估指标。

Method: 1. 指示LLM随机生成数字令牌；2. 通过测量观察到的数字分布与均匀分布的偏差来识别潜在的数值偏见；3. 在提示中加入下游任务定义以测量任务特定的潜在数字偏见；4. 在校正过程中，根据LLM的潜在数字偏见调整给定输入的令牌生成概率。

Result: 在LLM对齐评估、摘要评估、语义文本相似性(STS)和语义文本相关性(STR)四个任务上的实验表明，该方法优于无去偏处理的LLM及先前的校准方法。此外，证实了评分偏见因LLM、任务和分数范围而异。

Conclusion: 通过量化和校正潜在的数字偏见，可以有效减轻LLM评估器的评分偏见，且这种偏见具有情境依赖性，需针对性测量。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are often used as evaluators of text quality, known as LLM-as-a-Judge, which can outperform conventional automatic evaluation metrics that rely on reference texts. However, LLM evaluators tend to generate particular scores regardless of the context of the evaluated text, which is known as scoring bias. This study proposes a novel method to mitigate this scoring bias. An LLM is instructed to randomly generate number tokens, and the latent numerical bias of the LLM is identified by measuring the deviation of the observed distribution of numbers from the uniform distribution. A definition of a downstream task, for which an LLM evaluator is used, is added to the prompts for random number generation to measure task-specific latent number bias. In the evaluation by an LLM, the token generation probabilities for a given input are rectified considering the LLM's latent number bias. Results of the experiment on four different tasks, evaluation of LLM alignment, evaluation of summarization, Semantic Textual Similarity, and Semantic Textual Relatedness, demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms the baselines, including an LLM without debiasing and previous calibration methods. In addition, it is confirmed that scoring bias varies across LLMs, tasks, and score ranges, indicating the importance of measuring latent number bias as the case may be.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [From Interpretation to Compilation: A Compilation-Based Execution Engine for Semantic Operator Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06677)
*Wenkai Dong,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SemBaker是一种基于编译的语义算子执行引擎，通过LLM生成确定性Python函数替代逐条数据的解释执行，显著降低延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义算子系统采用基于解释的执行方式，需对每个数据项调用LLM进行推理，导致在处理循环中产生高昂的计算开销，使得延迟和费用随输入基数线性增长。

Method: 提出SemBaker作为外部插件，针对选定的过滤、映射和连接算子，仅调用一次LLM生成确定性的本地Python函数执行；结合基于成本的优化器路由算子执行方式，并支持流水线执行重叠。兼容Palimpzest、LOTUS、Nirvana和DocETL等系统。

Result: 在三个包含200个查询的QA工作负载中，相比基线方法，平均加速比为4.8至6.3倍，平均成本降低5.4至10.7倍，且处理质量保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 编译式执行能有效解决语义算子在大规模数据处理中的性能瓶颈，在保持高质量的同时大幅提升了效率并降低了使用成本。

Abstract: Semantic operators extend data processing with natural-language predicates. Existing semantic operator systems commonly execute these operators through interpretation-based execution: for every data item, an LLM interprets the operator predicate and directly produces the corresponding result. Although expressive, this design places expensive model invocations inside the data-processing loop, causing latency and monetary cost to scale with input cardinality.
  We present SemBaker, a compilation-based execution engine for semantic operator systems. SemBaker acts as an external plugin rather than replacing a backend's native execution. For selected semantic filters, maps, and joins, it invokes an LLM once to generate a deterministic Python function and executes that function locally without per-item LLM calls. A cost-based optimizer routes each operator to native or compiled execution, while compilation overlaps pipeline execution. SemBaker supports Palimpzest, LOTUS, Nirvana, and DocETL through thin adapters. Across three 200-query QA workloads, SemBaker achieves average speedups of 4.8 to 6.3 times and average cost reductions of 5.4 to 10.7 times, with competitive processing quality.

</details>


### [127] [Duration-constrained Interval Joins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06856)
*Naoya Ehara,Daichi Amagata*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的持续时间约束区间连接算法，解决了现有算法因忽略重叠时长而产生大量低效噪声数据的问题。通过引入最小重叠时长ε作为过滤条件，并结合两种优化技术，显著提升了处理效率，实验证明其优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区间连接算法虽然高效，但未考虑区间重叠的持续时间，导致返回包含极小重叠甚至无实质关联的区间对。这不仅为后续分析引入了噪声或不必要的数据，还增加了连接时间和计算开销。

Method: 提出了针对持续时间约束区间连接问题的新算法。给定两个区间集合R、S及最小重叠时长约束ε，仅返回重叠时长至少为ε的区间对。区别于先运行现有算法再过滤的低效方式，该算法直接剔除无效区间对，并应用了两种优化技术以提升执行效率。

Result: 在三个真实世界的时间间隔数据集上进行了广泛实验。结果表明，所提出的算法在处理持续时间约束区间连接任务时，性能明显优于现有的适用技术。

Conclusion: 通过直接处理持续时间约束而非事后过滤，结合特定的优化策略，可以有效解决传统区间连接中产生的噪声和效率低下问题，为需要精确时间重叠分析的数据库应用提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Many databases, including temporal, uncertain, spatial, and trajectory databases, use interval data, and interval joins are among the most frequently used operators. Many studies proposed efficient interval join algorithms, but they do not consider the overlap duration. They return any pairs of intervals, even if they overlap very slightly, e.g., with no essential correlation or relationship. Subsequent applications may suffer from such interval pairs, as they may be noise or unnecessary for the analysis. Furthermore, outputting such pairs also increases join time. To address the above issues, this paper addresses the problem of duration-constrained interval join. Given two interval collections $R$ and $S$ and an overlap duration constraint $ε$, this problem returns all interval pairs $(r,s)$ such that $r \in R$, $s \in S$, and the overlap duration between $r$ and $s$ is at least $ε$. A straightforward approach for this problem is to run a state-of-the-art interval join algorithm and then filter qualified interval pairs. However, this is inefficient, as it generates unnecessary interval pairs and incurs duration computations, which cannot overcome the above efficiency concern. We propose an efficient algorithm for this problem that removes the above drawback. Furthermore, we propose two optimization techniques to improve the efficiency of our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on three real-world interval datasets, and the results demonstrate that our algorithm outperforms existing techniques applicable to our problem.

</details>


### [128] [MIRA: Evidence-Verified Repair Memory for Text-to-SQL Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06950)
*Yining Liu,Chenyu Yang,Boyan Li,Rui Mao,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出MIRA，一种基于数据库证据的SQL纠错器，通过将历史纠错经验转化为可独立复用的记忆项，解决现有方法因粗粒度复用导致正确查询被错误修改的问题，显著提升了Text-to-SQL的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL代理生成的SQL常存在语义错误，而现有纠错方法将多个错误及其修复打包为粗粒度的经验进行复用，容易引入无关编辑，甚至将原本正确的查询变为错误，缺乏对“保留什么、何时激活、如何适配”的精细控制。

Method: 提出MIRA（Memory-Item Reuse and Adaptation），将历史纠错转换为独立的、可复用的“维修记忆项”。针对当前查询，通过问题和SQL检索相关记忆项，利用数据库证据验证并筛选支持的项，最后将其适配到当前SQL中。

Result: 在BIRD和ScienceBenchmark数据集的14个数据库上，对1,785个测试查询进行评估，MIRA分别使执行准确率提高了16.53%和8.78%。

Conclusion: MIRA通过细粒度的记忆项复用和基于数据库证据的适配机制，实现了可靠且高效的SQL纠错，有效解决了现有方法在经验复用中的副作用问题。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL agents still produce executable yet semantically incorrect SQL. A reliable SQL corrector must repair incorrect queries without corrupting correct ones. Confirmed corrections from the same database can be reused without parameter updates. Existing methods, however, often bundle multiple errors and their repairs into a single coarse-grained experience. Applying the entire experience can introduce irrelevant edits and turn an initially correct query into an incorrect one. Reliable reuse therefore depends on three decisions: what to retain from a historical correction, when to activate the resulting memory, and how to adapt it to the current SQL. We propose MIRA (Memory-Item Reuse and Adaptation), a pluggable SQL corrector that uses database evidence to guide memory reuse. MIRA converts historical corrections into independently reusable repair memory items. For each current query, it retrieves memory items using the question and SQL. It then checks each item against database evidence and adapts the supported items to the current SQL. We evaluate 1,785 test queries generated by three Text-to-SQL agents across 14 databases from BIRD and ScienceBenchmark. MIRA improves execution accuracy by 16.53% and 8.78% on BIRD and ScienceBenchmark, respectively.

</details>


### [129] [RECAST: A Region-Scoped Adaptive Index for Exact Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06962)
*Yining Liu,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RECAST的区域自适应索引，用于精确相似度搜索。它通过累积和重用已计算的“付费距离”来减少重复计算，并根据查询分布的变化动态调整索引结构，显著降低了距离计算量和查询时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确相似度搜索方法面临两个主要问题：一是基于预构建索引的方法无法适应查询流在特定区域的集中现象；二是现有的自适应索引（如AV-tree）虽然能根据查询动态构建索引，但丢弃了大量已计算的距离信息，且未能有效组织这些信息以供后续重用，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出了RECAST算法，这是一种区域范围的自适应索引。其核心机制包括：1) 维护查询区域并累积区域内已计算的“付费距离”，用于精确剪枝；2) 利用这些距离剪枝效果的变化来推断新查询是否仍集中在当前区域；3) 当查询分布发生偏移时，递归地将查询任务分发到子区域，确保付费距离仅在有效的区域内被积累和重用。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集和四种工作负载模式下的实验表明，RECAST的累计成本始终低于自适应基线和其他大多数预构建基线。与最先进的自适应基线AV-tree相比，RECAST将每次查询的距离计算量减少了高达64%，并将查询时间减少了高达46%。

Conclusion: RECAST通过有效地利用历史查询中已计算的距离信息并进行区域化的自适应管理，显著提高了精确相似度搜索的效率，证明了在查询分布非均匀情况下，重用已计算距离比单纯丢弃或静态索引更具优势。

Abstract: Similarity search in metric spaces is widely used in bioinformatics, data mining, and recommender systems. Exact similarity search is dominated by distance computations, while real query streams often concentrate in specific regions rather than spreading uniformly across the space. Pre-built indexes are constructed before the query stream and cannot adapt when queries concentrate in poorly served regions. Adaptive indexes such as AV-tree build the index from distances computed while answering queries, but discard many of those distances and do not organize the retained distances effectively for reuse. We propose RECAST, a region-scoped adaptive index for exact similarity search. RECAST maintains query regions, accumulates distances already computed while answering earlier queries (paid distances) within each region for exact pruning, and uses changes in their pruning effect to infer whether incoming queries remain concentrated in the current region. When queries shift, RECAST recursively dispatches query work to child regions, so paid distances are accumulated and reused only where they remain effective. On five real-world datasets under four workload patterns, RECAST achieves consistently lower cumulative cost than the adaptive baseline and most pre-built baselines, reducing per-query distance computations by up to 64% and query time by up to 46% compared with the state-of-the-art adaptive baseline AV-tree.

</details>


### [130] [Toward a Causal Data Management Ecosystem for Decision Making and Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07214)
*Dazhuo Qiu,Yingli Zhou,Amedeo Pachera,Angela Bonifati,Andrea Mauri*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文指出现代AI生态系统面临数据碎片化和相关性误导决策的问题，提出构建一个共享、持久且可查询的“因果世界系统”（CWS），通过引入显式的因果推理层，使智能体能够区分因果与相关，从而做出可靠、可预测后果的自主决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI已演变为包含多种模型和智能体的生态系统，其知识分散在异构数据源中。仅靠数据集成不足以解决决策问题，因为现有系统依赖相关性信号，导致在自主行动时产生混淆决策，缺乏对行动后果的预见性和可靠性。

Method: 提出构建“因果世界系统”（Causal World System, CWS）作为生态系统的显式因果层。该系统旨在整合异构数据，利用因果推理技术区分结果驱动因素与伴随变量，支持规范性分析和反事实分析。

Result: 确立了在AI生态系统中引入显式因果层的必要性，并提出了CWS的具体构想，旨在解决从相关性到因果性的跨越，提升系统的可信度和可靠性。

Conclusion: 为了实现可信赖和可靠的自主智能体，AI生态系统必须超越单纯的数据集成和相关性建模，通过构建CWS引入因果推理能力，以准确识别因果驱动因素并进行前瞻性决策分析。

Abstract: Modern AI is no longer a single model but an ecosystem: classical ML predictors, deep and multimodal models, large language models, and agents, each trained and tuned over different data sources and each producing outputs at scale that become inputs to the others. Operating such an ecosystem is fundamentally a data integration problem - the knowledge it depends on is fragmented across dozens of heterogeneous, independently governed sources that must be reconciled and continually maintained. Yet integration alone is not enough. The predictions these systems make are shaped by many interacting factors, and the events, decisions, and variables that drive an outcome are routinely entangled with the ones that merely accompany it; treated as a basis for action, such correlational signals invite confounded decisions. This becomes acute once agents act autonomously: to be trustworthy and reliable, an agent must anticipate the consequences of its actions, not merely extrapolate from what has co-occurred before. Causal reasoning is what closes this gap, distinguishing the drivers of an outcome from its correlates, and enabling prescriptive and counterfactual analysis over the ecosystem's data. We therefore argue that the integrated ecosystem needs an explicit causal layer, and we propose to build it as a shared, persistent, queryable Causal World System (CWS).

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Progressive Alignment of Recommender Foundation Model through Multi-Phase Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06792)
*Oseong Choi,Hoeinn Kim,Jihoon Lee,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种三阶段渐进式后训练框架（LP-FFT-RFT），将基础模型在推荐系统中的下游适应与业务指标对齐分离，通过线性探测、全量微调及强化微调提升推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一预训练基础模型通过监督微调适配下游任务时，优化的特定任务目标（如点击率）往往与实际决定推荐质量的业务指标不一致。

Method: 1. 适应阶段：分解为线性探测（LP）和全量微调（FFT）。LP在冻结的预训练表示空间中稳定下游头，FFT联合专业化整个模型。2. 对齐阶段：使用强化微调（RFT），基于学习到的奖励模型，利用密集隐式反馈训练策略，仅对奖励建模使用业务指标监督。

Result: 离线实验表明该框架优于单阶段替代方案，且基于奖励的对齐产生的服务策略强于直接使用奖励模型进行排序；大规模在线A/B测试显示其比传统非基础基线显著提升了生产环境的推荐质量。

Conclusion: 显式分离下游适应与业务指标对齐的渐进式后训练框架能有效提升基础模型在推荐系统中的实际业务表现。

Abstract: Foundation model(FM) for recommendation has shown strong ability to model long-horizon sequential user behavior. In practice, a single pretrained foundation model is often adapted to diverse downstream serving surfaces through Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT). However, optimizing task-specific objectives such as clicks or likes does not necessarily align the serving policy with the business metrics that determine recommendation quality.
  We propose a three-phase progressive post-training framework that explicitly separates downstream adaptation from business-metric alignment. The adaptation stage is decomposed into Linear Probing(LP) and Full Fine-Tuning(FFT): LP first stabilizes randomly initialized downstream heads within a frozen pretrained representation space, and FFT then jointly specializes the full model for the target task. On top of this stabilized policy, Reinforcement Fine-Tuning(RFT) aligns the model with practical business objectives using a learned reward model. Rather than directly optimizing the serving policy on sparse business targets, we train the policy on dense implicit feedback and use business-metric supervision only for reward modeling.
  Offline experiments show that the progressive LP-FFT-RFT framework outperforms single-phase alternatives, and that reward-based alignment yields a stronger serving policy than directly using the reward model itself for ranking. Large-scale online A/B tests further show that the proposed framework improves production recommendation quality over a conventional non-foundation baseline. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/webtoon/rec-fm-progressive-alignment

</details>


### [132] [Rhetorical-Role-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Legal Question Answering over Indian Supreme Court Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06828)
*Sayed Ayaan Ahmed Sha,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Anand Kumar Madasamy,Navya Binu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种针对印度最高法院判决的法律领域增强检索生成（RAG）框架，通过修辞分块、融合检索和重排序提升信息相关性，结合对话历史优化交互，在DeepEval评估中表现出高上下文召回率和答案相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协助从印度最高法院判决中进行交互式检索和推理，解决法律问答任务对大量上下文的需求，并提高法律AI系统的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 1. 增强型RAG框架：采用基于修辞的分块、融合式检索和交叉编码器重排序以提升检索相关性；2. 交互优化：利用对话历史、查询分类与重写理解用户意图；3. 结构特征整合：考虑法官姓名等法律文档结构要素以改善检索质量；4. 评估：使用DeepEval框架进行性能测试。

Result: 在上下文召回率（contextual recall）和答案相关性（answer relevancy）指标上表现强劲，证明该框架在处理需要大量上下文的法律问答任务中非常有效。

Conclusion: 领域特定的增强对于开发既可靠又可解释的法律AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: This research paper proposes a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) framework that is specific to the legal field in order to assist interactive retrieval and reason about judgments from the Supreme Court of India. The solution uses an enhanced version of RAG framework which consists of rhetorically based chunking, fusion-based retrieval, and cross encoder reranking methods to increase the relevancy of the information retrieved. In order to improve conversations, the proposed framework uses chat history along with query classification and rewriting in order to understand user intention from successive queries. Additionally, there are features that take into account structural aspects of legal documents, such as isolated names of judges that could have an impact on retrieval quality. The evaluation was done using the DeepEval framework and demonstrated strong performance on metrics including contextual recall and answer relevancy, which proves that the framework is very effective in dealing with legal question-answering tasks that require a lot of context. The results emphasize the importance of domain specific enhancements in developing legal AI systems that are both reliable and explainable.

</details>


### [133] [BZKO: An Ontology for the Card Index of German Post-War Compensation Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06918)
*Dilek Yargan,Jörg Waitelonis,Mahsa Vafaie,Harald Sack*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了BZK本体（BZKO），旨在对德国中央联邦卡索引（Bundeszentralkartei）中的纳粹迫害赔偿档案进行语义化整合与重用。该本体采用双层架构，结合BFO基础本体与RiC-O、PROV-O等档案标准，实现了历史数据的逻辑严谨性与互操作性，为数字人文基础设施提供了可复用的语义基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国中央联邦卡索引是记录纳粹迫害受害者及其亲属赔偿索赔的关键档案资源。为了实现这一具有历史意义的集合的语义丰富表示、集成和重用，需要解决历史档案数据在数字化环境下的标准化整合问题。

Method: 提出了一种用于历史档案数据的双层本体论方法：1. 分离本体论 grounded 领域语义与面向互操作性的扩展结构；2. 结合基于BFO（基本形式本体）的现实主义与档案标准（RiC-O, PROV-O, PiCo）；3. 通过这种组合实现保持溯源的语义集成，同时保持逻辑严谨性、模块化及跨数字人文基础设施的可复用性。

Result: 建立了一个可复用的语义基础，支持将Wiedergutmachung（赔偿）档案材料集成到数字人文基础设施中。该本体支持未来的知识图谱生成、本体验证，并能容纳额外的历史实体以及不确定的时间和空间信息。本体代码已开源。

Conclusion: BZKO本体成功地为历史赔偿档案的语义集成提供了方法论支持和工具基础，促进了历史数据在数字人文领域的深度挖掘与长期保存，并为处理复杂的历史不确定性信息奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Central Federal Card Index (Bundeszentralkartei) of Germany is a key archival resource documenting compensation claims submitted by victims of National Socialist persecution and their relatives, within the German Wiedergutmachung process. To enable semantically enriched representation, integration, and reuse of this historically significant collection, we present the BZK Ontology (BZKO). We propose a two-layer ontology for historical archival data that separates ontologically grounded domain semantics from interoperability-oriented extension constructs. The approach combines BFO-based realism with archival standards (RiC-O, PROV-O, PiCo), enabling provenance-preserving semantic integration, while maintaining logical rigor, modularity, and reuse across digital humanities infrastructures. The proposed approach establishes a reusable semantic foundation for the integration of Wiedergutmachung archival materials into digital humanities infrastructures and lays the groundwork for future knowledge graph generation, ontology validation, and the incorporation of additional historical entities and uncertain temporal and spatial information. The ontology is available on https://github.com/ISE-FIZKarlsruhe/bzko.

</details>


### [134] [From Classification to Recommendation: Empirical Analysis of Audio Embedding Models Application for Content-Based Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06928)
*Qingrui Li,Haowei Lou,Chengkai Huang,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种音频编码器在三种音乐推荐系统中的表现，发现音频-文本对齐和音乐域预训练表示在直接使用几何结构时更有效，而交互式序列训练能缩小编码器差异；同时指出增加语义ID容量并不总是提升生成式推荐系统性能，甚至可能引入不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练音频模型多针对掩码预测、对比学习或音频-文本对齐优化，其表征空间未必适合推荐系统。音乐推荐需捕捉受主观和行为依赖偏好的项目关系，且预训练音频嵌入在新兴的生成式推荐系统中的有效性尚未充分探索。

Method: 在基于内容、序列式和基于语义ID（Semantic-ID）的生成式三种音乐推荐系统中，系统评估了六种代表性音频编码器。进一步研究了残差量化设计（包括码本宽度、量化深度和保留的语义ID前缀）对保留推荐相关信息的影响。

Result: 在两个音乐推荐数据集上的实验表明：当直接使用预训练嵌入几何时，音频-文本对齐和音乐域表征通常更有效；交互式的序列训练显著降低了不同编码器之间的性能差异。此外，增加语义ID容量并未 consistently 提升生成式推荐系统性能，反而可能引入显著的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为现代音乐推荐系统中选择音频编码器和设计音频衍生的语义ID提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio representation models learned from large-scale corpora have achieved strong performance in audio classification and understanding. However, most existing models are optimized for objectives such as masked prediction, contrastive learning, or audio-text alignment, which do not necessarily produce representation spaces well-suited to recommender systems. Unlike classification, music recommender systems must capture item relationships shaped by subjective and behavior-dependent listener preferences. Although pretrained audio embeddings have been explored in conventional recommender systems, their effectiveness in the rapidly emerging paradigm of generative recommender systems remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically evaluate six representative audio encoders across three types of music recommender systems: content-based, sequential, and Semantic-ID-based generative recommender systems. We further investigate how residual-quantization design, including codebook width, quantization depth, and retained Semantic-ID prefixes, affects the preservation of recommendation-relevant information. Experiments on two music recommendation datasets show that audio-text-aligned and music-domain representations are generally more effective when pretrained embedding geometry is used directly, whereas interaction-based sequential training substantially reduces performance differences among encoders. We also find that increasing Semantic-ID capacity does not consistently improve generative recommender systems and may introduce substantial instability. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting audio encoders and designing audio-derived Semantic IDs for modern music recommender systems.

</details>


### [135] [Hierarchical Quantization with Domain-Adaptive Sparse Routing for Generative Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06997)
*Haiying He,Xiaopeng Li,Yuchen Gu,Kuo Cai,Bo Chen,Jingtong Gao,Yejing Wang,Derong Xu,Ruiming Tang,Guorui Zhou,Han Li,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HD-Rec，一种用于跨域推荐的统一生成式框架。通过分层领域感知量化器和领域自适应稀疏混合专家模块，解决异构物品语义和行为模式建模难题，在多基准测试中表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成式推荐（GenRec）扩展至跨域场景面临挑战，因为统一模型需适应不同领域的异构物品语义和行为模式。现有方法依赖全局共享表示或轻量级领域适配，在建模不同语义粒度下的异构模式时能力不足。

Method: 提出HD-Rec框架：1) 使用分层领域感知量化器，结合全局共享的粗粒度代码库和自适应路由的细粒度代码库构建语义标识符；2) 引入领域自适应稀疏混合专家模块，结合持续激活的共享专家和动态选择的专用专家；3) 开发跨粒度路由一致性目标，通过正则化使token级路由决策向item级共识靠拢，提升多token物品表示的一致性。

Result: 在三个公共跨域推荐基准上的实验表明，HD-Rec一致性地优于具有竞争力的序列推荐、生成式推荐和跨域推荐基线方法。

Conclusion: HD-Rec通过分层量化和混合专家机制有效解决了跨域推荐中的异构模式建模问题，证明了该统一生成式框架在提升推荐性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GenRec) represents a promising paradigm that achieves remarkable empirical success by encoding items as compact Semantic IDs (SIDs) and modeling user behavior via next-token prediction across diverse recommendation scenarios. Extending this paradigm to cross-domain recommendation is challenging because a unified model must accommodate heterogeneous item semantics and behavioral patterns across domains. Existing methods commonly rely on globally shared representations or lightweight domain adaptation, which may provide insufficient capacity for modeling heterogeneous patterns at different semantic granularities. To address these challenges, we propose HD-Rec, a unified generative framework for cross-domain recommendation. HD-Rec employs a hierarchical domain-aware quantizer that constructs semantic identifiers using globally shared coarse-level codebooks and adaptively routed fine-level codebooks. It further introduces a domain-adaptive sparse mixture-of-experts module that combines a continuously activated shared expert with a dynamically selected specialized expert. To improve the coherence of multi-token item representations, we develop a cross-granularity routing consistency objective that regularizes token-level routing decisions toward their item-level consensus. Experiments on three public cross-domain recommendation benchmarks show that HD-Rec consistently improves over competitive sequential, generative, and cross-domain recommendation baselines.

</details>


### [136] [MISO: Model-Internal-State-Guided Optimization for Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07035)
*Yongzhe Zhang,Xiaoyu Deng,Yifan He,Mengying Sun,Sheng Luo,Yijia Liu,Hao Yan,Zhuo Li,Yi Meng,Huiping Yao,Swathi Hrishikesh,Jing Chen,Dennis Choi,Steven Liu,Lexi Luo,Keyi Chen,Anish Khazane,Marcio Porto,Xiaoya Wang,Emmy Wang,Kangfu Zheng,Xingyuan Wang,Bilal Fadlallah,Gursharan Singh,Prabhakar Goyal,Alireza Vahdatpour,Santanu Kolay*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出模型内部状态优化（MISO）工作流，通过分析训练模型的参数、激活值等内部状态，自动生成可解释的局部优化建议，在广告排序案例中比传统方法更高效地提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的排名模型优化多依赖昂贵的试错法来确定组件的扩展、替换或退役，缺乏系统性指导，且手动调优与黑盒自动化搜索之间存在效率与透明度的平衡难题。

Method: MISO提取训练好的排名模型的内部状态（包括参数、激活值、梯度和归一化统计量），将其聚合为排名、对齐和比较信号，并转化为少量可解释的候选编辑操作；通过每轮重新训练后重复提取内部状态，支持适应数据分布变化的自适应优化工作流。

Result: 在广告排序案例研究中，MISO显著提升了标准化熵指标，同时所需的验证运行次数远少于专家驱动和黑盒缩放工作流，实现了介于手动调优与不透明自动搜索之间的实用平衡。

Conclusion: MISO提供了一种基于模型内部状态的系统化、适应性优化方法，能够以更少的计算资源实现高效的模型局部改进，为排名模型的持续优化提供了新的实践路径。

Abstract: Ranking models are repeatedly refined within established model families, yet the choice of which component to scale, replace, or retire is often guided by expensive trial-and-error. We present Model Internal State Optimization (MISO), a systems workflow that uses model internal states (MIS), including parameters, activations, gradients, and normalization statistics, to prioritize such local optimization decisions. MISO extracts MIS from a trained ranking model, aggregates them into ranking, alignment, and comparison signals, and converts those signals into a small set of interpretable candidate edits. Because MIS are re-extracted after each retraining cycle, MISO naturally supports an adaptive optimization workflow that tracks evolving model behavior as data distributions and system requirements shift over time. In an ads ranking case study, MISO improves normalized entropy while requiring substantially fewer validation runs than expert-driven and black-box scaling workflows, offering a practical middle ground between manual tuning and opaque automated search.

</details>


### [137] [Teacher Retains Full Tokens, Student Merges Efficiently: TM20K for E-Commerce Sequence Modeling in Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07055)
*Xinchun Li,Duoru Zheng,Wenlin Zhao,Ziyi Zhou,Jingxuan Tan,Huizhi Yang,Linlan Chen,Dongjian Wang,Dongyue Wang,Xiaosong Li,Hongyue Mao,Yaocheng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TM20K框架，通过全注意力机制和两阶段知识蒸馏，在保持高效的同时实现对20K超长用户行为序列的有效建模，已在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统中成功部署并提升业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统虽能利用超长行为序列提升体验，但面临训练效率低和服务吞吐量差的问题；传统压缩方法损失细粒度信息，而轻量级目标注意力结构无法充分提取序列特征，需在效果与效率间取得平衡。

Method: 提出名为TM20K的两阶段知识蒸馏框架：1）师生模型均采用全注意力机制而非纯目标序列注意力以有效扩展序列规模；2）对学生模型引入简单的Token合并策略，在保持可接受性能的同时显著压缩序列长度；3）对教师模型进行重度全序列训练，并通过知识蒸馏提升学生模型性能。

Result: 该范式在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统中部署，将电商序列长度扩展至20K，关键业务指标显著提升（如ADSS +1.036%），同时训练和服务成本基本保持不变（服务延迟仅增加5.6%）。

Conclusion: 通过结合全Transformer建模与两阶段知识蒸馏及Token合并技术，TM20K成功解决了超长序列建模中的效率与效果权衡问题，实现了大规模工业级应用中的性能突破。

Abstract: Benefiting from ultra-long behavior sequence modeling, existing recommender systems bring users a better experience via simultaneously considering their long-term and short-term interests. Nevertheless, extended sequence lengths introduce substantial burdens on training efficiency and serving throughput. Prior approaches typically utilize search-based or cluster-based compression on ultra-long sequences at the cost of fine-grained information, or rely on various lightweight target attention structures incapable of sufficient sequential feature extraction. In this paper, we balance the effectiveness and efficiency for ultra-long sequence modeling via full transformer modeling accompanied with a two-stage knowledge distillation framework. First, both teacher and student models take the full attention mechanism rather than pure target-sequence attention for effective sequence scaling. For student models, we propose several simple yet well-motivated token merge approaches, significantly compressing the sequence length while maintaining an acceptable performance. Then, a one-time teacher is heavily trained with full sequence tokens, further boosting the performance of student models via knowledge distillation. The proposed paradigm named TM20K has been successfully deployed in ByteDance's e-commerce advertising recommender system that extends the e-commerce sequence length to 20K, delivering substantial improvements in key business metrics (e.g., ADSS +1.036\%) while keeping the training and serving cost nearly the same as the online state-of-the-art model (e.g., serving latency only +5.6\%).

</details>


### [138] [Invisible to the Machine: Auditing AI Restaurant, Cafe, and Bar Recommendation Against a Complete Market Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07069)
*Vladimir Pitenin*

Main category: cs.IR
